import Phaser from 'phaser';
import { BattleChannel } from '../utils/battleChannel';
import { BattleEvent } from '../types/BattleTypes';
import { getSafeSelectedCharacter } from '../utils/character';
import { supabase } from '../utils/supabase';
import BaseScene from './BaseScene';

/**
 * 대전 매칭 씬 — 상태별 UI 전환
 *
 * 상태: MENU → CREATE_WAITING / JOIN_INPUT → JOIN_WAITING → COUNTDOWN → BATTLE
 */
export default class BattleMatchScene extends BaseScene {
  private battleChannel: BattleChannel | null = null;
  private inputElement: HTMLInputElement | null = null;
  private isHost: boolean = false;
  private myReady: boolean = false;
  private opponentReady: boolean = false;
  private countdownStarted: boolean = false;
  private readyRetryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private userId: string = '';

  // 상태별 UI 요소를 그룹으로 관리 → 상태 전환 시 일괄 파괴
  private uiGroup: Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject[] = [];
  private uiTimers: Phaser.Time.TimerEvent[] = [];

  constructor() {
    super('BattleMatchScene');
  }

  preload() {
    if (!this.textures.exists('background2')) {
      this.load.image('background2', 'assets/backgrounds/background2.webp');
    }
  }

  async create() {
    super.create();

    // 배경 (모든 상태에서 유지)
    const bg = this.add.image(200, 300, 'background2');
    bg.setDisplaySize(400, 600);
    this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0.6);

    // 타이틀 (모든 상태에서 유지)
    this.add.text(200, 60, '⚔️ 대전 모드', {
      fontSize: '32px',
      color: '#ff4444',
      fontStyle: 'bold',
      stroke: '#000',
      strokeThickness: 5,
    }).setOrigin(0.5);

    this.add.text(200, 105, 'EXTREME 난이도로 1대1 대전!', {
      fontSize: '14px',
      color: '#cccccc',
      stroke: '#000',
      strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5);

    // 뒤로가기 (모든 상태에서 유지)
    const backBtn = this.add.text(200, 560, '← 메인 메뉴', {
      fontSize: '18px',
      color: '#aaaaaa',
      stroke: '#000',
      strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
    backBtn.on('pointerover', () => backBtn.setColor('#ffffff'));
    backBtn.on('pointerout', () => backBtn.setColor('#aaaaaa'));
    backBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.goBack());

    // 유저 ID
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    this.userId = data.user?.id ?? `anon-${Date.now()}`;

    // 초기 상태: 메뉴
    this.showMenu();
  }

  /** 현재 상태의 UI 요소 일괄 파괴 */
  private clearUI() {
    this.removeInput();
    for (const obj of this.uiGroup) {
      if (obj.active) obj.destroy();
    }
    this.uiGroup = [];
    // TimerEvent는 GameObject가 아니므로 별도 정리
    for (const timer of this.uiTimers) {
      timer.remove();
    }
    this.uiTimers = [];
  }

  /** UI 요소를 그룹에 추가 (나중에 일괄 파괴용) */
  private ui<T extends Phaser.GameObjects.GameObject>(obj: T): T {
    this.uiGroup.push(obj);
    return obj;
  }

  // ─── 상태 1: 메뉴 (방 만들기 / 방 참가 선택) ─────────────────────

  private showMenu() {
    this.clearUI();

    // 방 만들기 버튼
    const createBtn = this.ui(this.add.rectangle(200, 250, 260, 70, 0xe74c3c));
    createBtn.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 250, '🏠 방 만들기', {
      fontSize: '22px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
    }).setOrigin(0.5));
    createBtn.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
    createBtn.on('pointerover', () => createBtn.setAlpha(0.8));
    createBtn.on('pointerout', () => createBtn.setAlpha(1));
    createBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.handleCreateRoom());

    // 방 참가 버튼
    const joinBtn = this.ui(this.add.rectangle(200, 350, 260, 70, 0x3498db));
    joinBtn.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 350, '🔗 방 참가', {
      fontSize: '22px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
    }).setOrigin(0.5));
    joinBtn.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
    joinBtn.on('pointerover', () => joinBtn.setAlpha(0.8));
    joinBtn.on('pointerout', () => joinBtn.setAlpha(1));
    joinBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.showJoinInput());
  }

  // ─── 상태 2a: 방 생성 대기 ─────────────────────────────────────

  private async handleCreateRoom() {
    if (this.battleChannel) return;
    this.isHost = true;
    this.myReady = false;
    this.opponentReady = false;
    this.countdownStarted = false;

    const code = BattleChannel.generateRoomCode();
    this.battleChannel = new BattleChannel();
    this.battleChannel.createRoom(code, this.userId);
    this.setupChannelListeners();
    await this.battleChannel.subscribe();

    this.showCreateWaiting(code);
  }

  private showCreateWaiting(code: string) {
    this.clearUI();

    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 200, '초대 코드', {
      fontSize: '16px', color: '#aaaaaa',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 260, code, {
      fontSize: '56px', color: '#FFD700', fontStyle: 'bold',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 6, letterSpacing: 10,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 330, '상대방에게 코드를 알려주세요', {
      fontSize: '15px', color: '#ffffff',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    // 대기 애니메이션 점
    const dots = this.ui(this.add.text(200, 370, '대기 중', {
      fontSize: '16px', color: '#888888',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    let dotCount = 0;
    this.uiTimers.push(
      this.time.addEvent({
        delay: 500,
        loop: true,
        callback: () => {
          dotCount = (dotCount + 1) % 4;
          if (dots.active) dots.setText('대기 중' + '.'.repeat(dotCount));
        },
      })
    );
  }

  // ─── 상태 2b: 코드 입력 (방 참가) ──────────────────────────────

  private showJoinInput() {
    this.clearUI();

    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 200, '방 코드 입력', {
      fontSize: '18px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    // Phaser 캔버스 좌표 → DOM 좌표 변환
    const canvas = this.game.canvas;
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scaleX = rect.width / 400;
    const scaleY = rect.height / 600;

    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'text';
    input.maxLength = 4;
    input.placeholder = 'CODE';
    input.style.cssText = `
      position: fixed;
      left: ${rect.left + 200 * scaleX}px;
      top: ${rect.top + 270 * scaleY}px;
      transform: translateX(-50%);
      width: ${Math.round(200 * scaleX)}px;
      height: ${Math.round(55 * scaleY)}px;
      font-size: ${Math.round(30 * scaleY)}px;
      text-align: center;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      border: 3px solid #FFD700;
      border-radius: 12px;
      background: #1a1a2e;
      color: #FFD700;
      font-weight: bold;
      letter-spacing: ${Math.round(10 * scaleX)}px;
      outline: none;
      z-index: 9999;
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    input.focus();
    this.inputElement = input;

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      input.value = input.value.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '');
    });

    // 에러 메시지 영역
    const errorText = this.ui(this.add.text(200, 330, '', {
      fontSize: '14px', color: '#ff4444',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    // 참가 버튼
    const joinBtn = this.ui(this.add.rectangle(200, 390, 200, 55, 0x27ae60));
    joinBtn.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 390, '참가', {
      fontSize: '22px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
    }).setOrigin(0.5));
    joinBtn.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
    joinBtn.on('pointerover', () => joinBtn.setAlpha(0.8));
    joinBtn.on('pointerout', () => joinBtn.setAlpha(1));

    const doJoin = async () => {
      const code = input.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      if (code.length !== 4) {
        errorText.setText('4자리 코드를 입력하세요');
        return;
      }

      errorText.setText('');
      this.isHost = false;
      this.myReady = false;
      this.opponentReady = false;
      this.countdownStarted = false;
      this.battleChannel = new BattleChannel();
      this.battleChannel.joinRoom(code, this.userId);
      this.setupChannelListeners();
      await this.battleChannel.subscribe();

      this.showJoinWaiting(code);
    };

    joinBtn.on('pointerdown', doJoin);
    input.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') doJoin();
    });

    // 뒤로 (메뉴로)
    const backText = this.ui(this.add.text(200, 470, '← 돌아가기', {
      fontSize: '16px', color: '#888888',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true }));
    backText.on('pointerover', () => backText.setColor('#ffffff'));
    backText.on('pointerout', () => backText.setColor('#888888'));
    backText.on('pointerdown', () => this.showMenu());
  }

  // ─── 상태 3: 참가 후 대기 ──────────────────────────────────────

  private showJoinWaiting(_code: string) {
    // 구독 완료 → 즉시 ready 전송
    this.doSendReady();
  }

  // ─── 채널 이벤트 ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  private setupChannelListeners() {
    if (!this.battleChannel) return;

    // Presence join — 호스트일 때 상대 입장 UI 갱신 + 즉시 ready 전송
    this.battleChannel.onPresenceJoin(() => {
      if (this.isHost && !this.myReady) {
        this.doSendReady();
      }
    });

    this.battleChannel.onPresenceLeave(() => {
      this.opponentReady = false;
      this.ui(this.add.text(200, 420, '⚠️ 상대방이 나갔습니다', {
        fontSize: '16px', color: '#ff4444',
        stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
      }).setOrigin(0.5));
    });

    // READY 수신 = 상대가 확실히 존재 + 준비 완료
    // Presence join보다 READY가 먼저 올 수 있으므로, 여기서도 자신의 ready를 보냄
    this.battleChannel.onEvent(BattleEvent.READY, () => {
      this.opponentReady = true;
      // 아직 내 ready를 안 보냈으면 즉시 보냄 (Presence 이벤트 누락 대비)
      if (!this.myReady) {
        this.doSendReady();
      }
      this.checkBothReady();
    });

    this.battleChannel.onEvent(BattleEvent.GAME_START, () => {
      if (!this.countdownStarted) {
        this.countdownStarted = true;
        this.showCountdown();
      }
    });
  }

  /** ready 전송 (UI 상태와 분리 — 어디서든 호출 가능) */
  private doSendReady() {
    if (!this.battleChannel || this.myReady) return;
    this.myReady = true;
    const charId = getSafeSelectedCharacter();
    this.battleChannel.sendReady(charId);

    // UI 갱신: 현재 화면 정리 → 대기 화면
    this.clearUI();
    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 270, '✅ 준비 완료!', {
      fontSize: '24px', color: '#00ff00', fontStyle: 'bold',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 4,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));
    this.ui(this.add.text(200, 320, '상대를 기다리는 중...', {
      fontSize: '16px', color: '#ffffff',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 3,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    // READY를 주기적으로 재전송 — 네트워크/타이밍 이슈로 상대가 수신 못했을 때 대비
    // setInterval 사용 (Phaser 타이머는 백그라운드 탭에서 멈추므로)
    this.clearReadyRetry();
    this.readyRetryTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (this.countdownStarted || !this.battleChannel) {
        this.clearReadyRetry();
        return;
      }
      this.battleChannel.sendReady(charId);
    }, 1500);

    this.checkBothReady();
  }

  private clearReadyRetry() {
    if (this.readyRetryTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.readyRetryTimer);
      this.readyRetryTimer = null;
    }
  }

  private checkBothReady() {
    if (this.myReady && this.opponentReady && this.isHost && !this.countdownStarted) {
      this.countdownStarted = true;
      this.battleChannel?.sendGameStart(Date.now());
      this.showCountdown();
    }
  }

  // ─── 상태 4: 카운트다운 ────────────────────────────────────────

  private showCountdown() {
    this.clearUI();

    let count = 3;
    const countText = this.ui(this.add.text(200, 280, `${count}`, {
      fontSize: '90px', color: '#ff4444', fontStyle: 'bold',
      stroke: '#000', strokeThickness: 8,
    }).setOrigin(0.5));

    const timer = this.time.addEvent({
      delay: 1000,
      callback: () => {
        count--;
        if (count > 0) {
          (countText as Phaser.GameObjects.Text).setText(`${count}`);
        } else {
          (countText as Phaser.GameObjects.Text).setText('GO!');
          (countText as Phaser.GameObjects.Text).setColor('#00ff00');
          timer.remove();
          this.time.delayedCall(500, () => this.goToBattle());
        }
      },
      repeat: 2,
    });
  }

  // ─── 씬 전환 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private goToBattle() {
    this.clearReadyRetry();
    const roomCode = this.battleChannel?.code ?? '';
    const userId = this.userId;

    // 동기식 채널 제거: supabase.channel()이 싱글턴이므로
    // removeChannel을 먼저 호출해야 BattleGameScene에서 새 채널 생성 가능
    if (this.battleChannel) {
      this.battleChannel.destroyImmediate();
      this.battleChannel = null;
    }

    this.scene.start('BattleGameScene', { roomCode, userId });
  }

  private removeInput() {
    if (this.inputElement) {
      this.inputElement.remove();
      this.inputElement = null;
    }
  }

  private async goBack() {
    this.clearReadyRetry();
    this.removeInput();
    if (this.battleChannel) {
      await this.battleChannel.destroy();
      this.battleChannel = null;
    }
    this.scene.start('ModeSelectScene');
  }

  shutdown() {
    this.clearReadyRetry();
    this.removeInput();
  }
}
```

/**
 * 1대1 대전 모드 타입 정의
 */

// 대전 상태 (as const — enum 금지)
export const BattleState = {
  WAITING: 'waiting',
  READY: 'ready',
  COUNTDOWN: 'countdown',
  PLAYING: 'playing',
  FINISHED: 'finished',
} as const;
export type BattleState = typeof BattleState[keyof typeof BattleState];

// 대전 결과
export const BattleResult = {
  WIN: 'win',
  LOSE: 'lose',
  DISCONNECT: 'disconnect',
} as const;
export type BattleResult = typeof BattleResult[keyof typeof BattleResult];

// ── Broadcast 이벤트 Payload 타입 ──

export interface ReadyPayload {
  userId: string;
  characterId: string;
}

export interface GameStartPayload {
  timestamp: number;
}

export interface SendPoopPayload {
  count: number;
}

export interface ScoreUpdatePayload {
  score: number;
}

export interface GameOverPayload {
  userId: string;
  finalScore: number;
}

// Broadcast 이벤트 이름
export const BattleEvent = {
  READY: 'ready',
  GAME_START: 'game-start',
  SEND_POOP: 'send-poop',
  SCORE_UPDATE: 'score-update',
  GAME_OVER: 'game-over',
} as const;
export type BattleEvent = typeof BattleEvent[keyof typeof BattleEvent];
---
import { type RealtimeChannel } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabase } from './supabase';
import {
  BattleEvent,
  type ReadyPayload,
  type GameStartPayload,
  type SendPoopPayload,
  type ScoreUpdatePayload,
  type GameOverPayload,
} from '../types/BattleTypes';

type BattlePayloadMap = {
  [BattleEvent.READY]: ReadyPayload;
  [BattleEvent.GAME_START]: GameStartPayload;
  [BattleEvent.SEND_POOP]: SendPoopPayload;
  [BattleEvent.SCORE_UPDATE]: ScoreUpdatePayload;
  [BattleEvent.GAME_OVER]: GameOverPayload;
};

/**
 * Supabase Realtime Broadcast 채널 래퍼
 *
 * ⚠️ Supabase Realtime은 `.on()` 리스너를 `.subscribe()` 전에 등록해야 합니다.
 * 따라서 채널 생성(createRoom/joinRoom) → 리스너 등록(onEvent 등) → 구독(subscribe) 순서로 호출하세요.
 */
export class BattleChannel {
  private channel: RealtimeChannel | null = null;
  private roomCode: string = '';
  private userId: string = '';

  /** 4자리 랜덤 방 코드 생성 */
  static generateRoomCode(): string {
    const chars = 'REDACTED'; // 혼동 문자(I,O,0,1) 제외
    let code = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
    }
    return code;
  }

  /** 방 생성 (호스트) — 채널만 생성, subscribe는 별도 호출 */
  createRoom(code: string, userId: string): void {
    this.roomCode = code;
    this.userId = userId;
    this.createChannel();
  }

  /** 방 참가 (게스트) — 채널만 생성, subscribe는 별도 호출 */
  joinRoom(code: string, userId: string): void {
    this.roomCode = code;
    this.userId = userId;
    this.createChannel();
  }

  /** 채널 객체만 생성 (구독하지 않음) */
  private createChannel(): void {
    this.channel = supabase.channel(`battle-room:${this.roomCode}`, {
      config: {
        broadcast: { self: false },
        presence: { key: this.userId },
      },
    });
  }

  /**
   * 리스너 등록 완료 후 호출 — 실제 WebSocket 구독 시작 + Presence track
   * 반드시 onEvent/onPresenceJoin/onPresenceLeave 등록 후에 호출하세요.
   */
  async subscribe(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.channel) return;
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this.channel!.subscribe(async (status) => {
        if (status === 'SUBSCRIBED') {
          await this.channel!.track({ userId: this.userId, joinedAt: Date.now() });
          resolve();
        }
      });
    });
  }

  /** Broadcast 이벤트 리스너 등록 (subscribe 전에 호출) */
  onEvent<E extends BattleEvent>(event: E, callback: (payload: BattlePayloadMap[E]) => void): void {
    if (!this.channel) return;
    this.channel.on('broadcast', { event }, (msg) => {
      callback(msg.payload as BattlePayloadMap[E]);
    });
  }

  /** Presence 동기화 — 상대 참가 감지 (subscribe 전에 호출) */
  onPresenceJoin(callback: (userId: string) => void): void {
    if (!this.channel) return;
    this.channel.on('presence', { event: 'join' }, ({ newPresences }) => {
      for (const p of newPresences) {
        const uid = (p as Record<string, unknown>).userId as string;
        if (uid && uid !== this.userId) {
          callback(uid);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  /** Presence 동기화 — 상대 이탈 감지 (subscribe 전에 호출) */
  onPresenceLeave(callback: (userId: string) => void): void {
    if (!this.channel) return;
    this.channel.on('presence', { event: 'leave' }, ({ leftPresences }) => {
      for (const p of leftPresences) {
        const uid = (p as Record<string, unknown>).userId as string;
        if (uid && uid !== this.userId) {
          callback(uid);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  /** 현재 Presence 상태에서 상대방이 있는지 확인 */
  getOpponentPresence(): string | null {
    if (!this.channel) return null;
    const state = this.channel.presenceState();
    for (const key of Object.keys(state)) {
      if (key !== this.userId) return key;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // ── 이벤트 송신 메서드 ──

  sendReady(characterId: string): void {
    this.broadcast(BattleEvent.READY, { userId: this.userId, characterId });
  }

  sendGameStart(timestamp: number): void {
    this.broadcast(BattleEvent.GAME_START, { timestamp });
  }

  sendPoop(count: number): void {
    this.broadcast(BattleEvent.SEND_POOP, { count });
  }

  sendScoreUpdate(score: number): void {
    this.broadcast(BattleEvent.SCORE_UPDATE, { score });
  }

  sendGameOver(finalScore: number): void {
    this.broadcast(BattleEvent.GAME_OVER, { userId: this.userId, finalScore });
  }

  private broadcast<E extends BattleEvent>(event: E, payload: BattlePayloadMap[E]): void {
    if (!this.channel) return;
    this.channel.send({
      type: 'broadcast',
      event,
      payload,
    });
  }

  /** 채널 정리 (비동기 — untrack 후 채널 제거) */
  async destroy(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.channel) {
      await this.channel.untrack();
      supabase.removeChannel(this.channel);
      this.channel = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * 채널 즉시 제거 (동기 — 씬 전환 전 호출용)
   *
   * supabase.channel()은 싱글턴이므로 removeChannel을 먼저 호출해야
   * 다음 씬에서 같은 이름으로 새 채널을 생성할 수 있다.
   */
  destroyImmediate(): void {
    if (this.channel) {
      supabase.removeChannel(this.channel);
      this.channel = null;
    }
  }

  get code(): string {
    return this.roomCode;
  }

  get myUserId(): string {
    return this.userId;
  }
}
```

Now I have enough information. Let me compile my findings.

## Code Reuse Opportunities - Final Report

Based on my thorough analysis of the changed files, here are the real findings with code reuse opportunities:

### 1. **CONFIRMED: Redundant `createRoom()` and `joinRoom()` in BattleChannel**

**File**: `src/utils/battleChannel.ts` (lines 42-53)

Both methods are functionally identical:
```typescript
createRoom(code: string, userId: string): void {
  this.roomCode = code;
  this.userId = userId;
  this.createChannel();  // ← both call the same private method
}

joinRoom(code: string, userId: string): void {
  this.roomCode = code;
  this.userId = userId;
  this.createChannel();  // ← same implementation
}
```

**Finding**: They could be replaced with a single private `_setRoomContext()` method called by both. The semantic difference exists only at the call site, not in implementation.

---

### 2. **CONFIRMED: Nearly Identical Presence Event Handlers**

**File**: `src/utils/battleChannel.ts` (lines 90-113)

`onPresenceJoin()` and `onPresenceLeave()` follow the exact same pattern:
```typescript
// Lines 90-99 (onPresenceJoin)
this.channel.on('presence', { event: 'join' }, ({ newPresences }) => {
  for (const p of newPresences) {
    const uid = (p as Record<string, unknown>).userId as string;
    if (uid && uid !== this.userId) {
      callback(uid);  // ← callback invoked
    }
  }
});

// Lines 104-112 (onPresenceLeave)  
this.channel.on('presence', { event: 'leave' }, ({ leftPresences }) => {
  for (const p of leftPresences) {
    const uid = (p as Record<string, unknown>).userId as string;
    if (uid && uid !== this.userId) {
      callback(uid);  // ← callback invoked
    }
  }
});
```

**Difference**: Only the event name (`'join'` vs `'leave'`) and the array property name (`newPresences` vs `leftPresences`) differ. Could be unified with a private helper.

---

### 3. **CONFIRMED: Button Creation Pattern Duplication**

**Files**: `src/scenes/BattleMatchScene.ts` and `src/scenes/ModeSelectScene.ts`

**BattleMatchScene** (lines 106-114, 117-125, 237-244, 272-278):
```typescript
// Pattern repeats 3+ times: rectangle + text + setInteractive + hover/click handlers
const createBtn = this.ui(this.add.rectangle(200, 250, 260, 70, 0xe74c3c));
createBtn.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
this.ui(this.add.text(200, 250, '🏠 방 만들기', { ... }));
createBtn.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
createBtn.on('pointerover', () => createBtn.setAlpha(0.8));
createBtn.on('pointerout', () => createBtn.setAlpha(1));
createBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.handleCreateRoom());
```

**ModeSelectScene** (lines 74-100, 102-135):
```typescript
// Similar pattern, already refactored into `createModeButton()` and `createBattleButton()`
// but `createBattleButton()` uses different hover styles than `createModeButton()`
```

**Issue**: BattleMatchScene creates buttons inline multiple times (lines 106-114 for create button, 117-125 for join button, 237-244 for join confirmation, 272-278 for back), while ModeSelectScene already uses helper methods (`createModeButton`, `createBattleButton`). BattleMatchScene could benefit from extracting a `createStyledButton()` helper.

---

### 4. **DEAD CODE: `BattleState` Type Definition**

**File**: `src/types/BattleTypes.ts` (lines 6-13)

```typescript
export const BattleState = {
  WAITING: 'waiting',
  READY: 'ready',
  COUNTDOWN: 'countdown',
  PLAYING: 'playing',
  FINISHED: 'finished',
} as const;
export type BattleState = typeof BattleState[keyof typeof BattleState];
```

**Finding**: Grep confirms `BattleState` is defined only in `src/types/BattleTypes.ts` and is **never imported or used anywhere else in the codebase**. It's dead code. The actual state management in BattleMatchScene uses `myReady`, `opponentReady`, `countdownStarted`, and `isHost` flags instead of a unified `BattleState` enum.

---

### 5. **Object Pool Pattern Consistency Issue**

**Files**: `src/scenes/GameScene.ts` vs `src/scenes/BattleGameScene.ts` (line 241-246)

GameScene pattern (standard):
```typescript
const poop = this.poops.get() as Poop;
if (!poop) continue;
poop.reinit(x, y, this.difficulty);
if (poop.body) {
  poop.body.velocity.y = fallSpeed;
}
```

BattleGameScene pattern (lines 241-246):
```typescript
const poop = this.poops.get() as Poop;
if (!poop) continue;
poop.reinit(x, y, Difficulty.EXTREME);  // ← passes difficulty explicitly
if (poop.body) {
  poop.body.velocity.y = fallSpeed * 1.2;  // ← different velocity multiplier
}
```

**Finding**: This is intentional (battle-specific behavior), not a code reuse issue. However, the pattern is consistent with GameScene's approach, so no refactoring needed.

---

### 6. **Closure vs Method Pattern**

**File**: `src/scenes/BattleMatchScene.ts` (lines 246-269)

`doJoin` is a closure defined inside `showJoinInput()` rather than a class method:
```typescript
const doJoin = async () => {
  // Implementation
};
joinBtn.on('pointerdown', doJoin);
input.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') doJoin();
});
```

This is functional but creates a new function instance per call to `showJoinInput()`. Compare with `doSendReady()` (line 328) which is a proper class method. Not a reuse issue, but inconsistent pattern.

---

## Summary

| Issue | Severity | Type |
|-------|----------|------|
| `createRoom`/`joinRoom` redundancy | Medium | Code duplication |
| Presence event handlers redundancy | Medium | Code duplication |
| Button creation pattern (BattleMatchScene) | Medium | Missing abstraction |
| `BattleState` unused type | Low | Dead code |
| `doJoin` closure pattern | Low | Style inconsistency |

## Code Review: BattleMatchScene.ts

I've identified **3 real issues** and **2 non-issues**. Here's the detailed analysis:

### ⚠️ **REAL ISSUES**

#### **Issue 1: Missing `shutdown()` cleanup — battleChannel leak on unexpected scene exit**

**Severity: HIGH**

**Location:** Line 436-439 (`shutdown()` method)

**Problem:**
```typescript
shutdown() {
  this.clearReadyRetry();
  this.removeInput();
  // Missing: if (this.battleChannel) ...
}
```

When Phaser shuts down the scene (e.g., browser refresh, manual scene.stop(), unhandled error), `shutdown()` is called but **does not clean up `battleChannel`**. The channel remains subscribed and holding WebSocket connections in memory.

You have two explicit cleanup paths (`goBack()` uses async `destroy()`, `goToBattle()` uses sync `destroyImmediate()`), but `shutdown()` misses both. This causes:
- WebSocket leak on unexpected exits (browser back, manual navigation, error states)
- Presence left in Supabase (stale "user joined" records)
- Memory leak if scene is restarted multiple times

**Fix:**
```typescript
shutdown() {
  this.clearReadyRetry();
  this.removeInput();
  // Sync cleanup only — don't await in shutdown()
  if (this.battleChannel) {
    this.battleChannel.destroyImmediate();
    this.battleChannel = null;
  }
}
```

---

#### **Issue 2: `create()` is async — state unavailable during render**

**Severity: MEDIUM**

**Location:** Line 37 (`async create()`)

**Problem:**
```typescript
async create() {
  super.create();
  // ...
  const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();  // Line 73
  this.userId = data.user?.id ?? `anon-${Date.now()}`;
  this.showMenu();  // Line 77
}
```

Phaser **does not await** `create()`. The render loop and update loop start immediately while the async operation is in-flight. For ~100ms (network latency), the scene renders with:
- `userId = ''` (empty string)
- `showMenu()` not yet called (blank canvas except background)

While not catastrophic (auth completes before user input), this creates a visual flicker and technically violates the Phaser lifecycle contract.

**Better approach:**
```typescript
create() {
  super.create();
  // Synchronous setup
  const bg = this.add.image(200, 300, 'background2');
  bg.setDisplaySize(400, 600);
  
  // ... (background + title + back button)
  
  // Init from cache or use default
  this.userId = this.cache.json.get('userId') ?? `anon-${Date.now()}`;
  this.showMenu();
  
  // Async auth in background (no await)
  supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {
    if (data.user?.id) {
      this.userId = data.user.id;
    }
  });
}
```

Or use **Phaser's scene data instead of instance variables** for async-filled values, then subscribe to them in UI methods.

---

#### **Issue 3: Loose async invariant in `handleCreateRoom()` — race condition if clicked twice quickly**

**Severity: MEDIUM**

**Location:** Line 130-144

**Problem:**
```typescript
private async handleCreateRoom() {
  if (this.battleChannel) return;  // Line 131 — guard, but
  this.isHost = true;
  this.myReady = false;
  this.opponentReady = false;
  this.countdownStarted = false;

  const code = BattleChannel.generateRoomCode();
  this.battleChannel = new BattleChannel();
  this.battleChannel.createRoom(code, this.userId);
  this.setupChannelListeners();
  await this.battleChannel.subscribe();  // Line 141 — async, unguarded

  this.showCreateWaiting(code);
}
```

**Scenario:** User clicks "방 만들기" twice in rapid succession:
1. Click 1: `if (this.battleChannel)` → null, passes guard
2. `this.battleChannel = new BattleChannel()` assigned
3. Click 2: `if (this.battleChannel)` → now non-null, returns ✓ (prevented)
4. But Click 1's `subscribe()` is still in-flight...

If Click 1 completes `subscribe()` *after* the UI state changes (e.g., scene transitions), you'll have duplicate subscriptions or listeners.

**Better fix:** Set a flag to prevent re-entry:
```typescript
private creatingRoom: boolean = false;

private async handleCreateRoom() {
  if (this.battleChannel || this.creatingRoom) return;  // Prevent re-entry during async
  this.creatingRoom = true;
  
  try {
    this.isHost = true;
    // ... rest of code
    await this.battleChannel!.subscribe();
    this.showCreateWaiting(code);
  } finally {
    this.creatingRoom = false;
  }
}
```

Same risk exists in `doJoin` closure (line 246). Same fix applies.

---

### ✓ **NON-ISSUES** (False Positives)

#### **1. `showJoinWaiting(_code: string)` ignores parameter**

**Not a real issue.** The parameter is there because:
- `handleCreateRoom()` calls `this.showCreateWaiting(code)` and displays the code
- `doJoin()` calls `this.showJoinWaiting(code)` by symmetry
- But after joining, the UI doesn't need to display the code (player already knows it)

The `_code` prefix correctly signals "unused parameter" and prevents linters from complaining. This is standard TypeScript practice. ✓

---

#### **2. `countdownStarted` flag redundancy**

**Not a real issue.** This flag:
- Guards against double-countdown in two places:
  - Line 320-323: `onEvent(BattleEvent.GAME_START)` checks it
  - Line 367: `checkBothReady()` checks it
  
- Is set by both the host (via `checkBothReady()`) and guest (via `GAME_START` event)
- Without it, if both fire at once or in quick succession, `showCountdown()` would be called twice

**Could simplify to:** `if (this.scene.isRunning('BattleMatchScene'))` to check if countdown already started, but the explicit flag is clearer and has zero performance cost. The redundancy is intentional safety, not a flaw. ✓

---

### Summary

| Issue | Severity | Fix Type |
|-------|----------|----------|
| **Leak:** `shutdown()` doesn't destroy channel | HIGH | Add `destroyImmediate()` call |
| **Flicker:** `create()` is async | MEDIUM | Move auth into background or use scene data |
| **Race condition:** `handleCreateRoom()` async re-entry | MEDIUM | Add `creatingRoom` flag + try/finally |
| `_code` unused param | ✓ Non-issue | Intentional; correctly marked |
| `countdownStarted` redundancy | ✓ Non-issue | Intentional safety measure |

## Code Review Report: BattleGameScene Efficiency Issues

Based on my analysis of the battle system code, I've identified **4 actual issues** (1-4 are real problems; question 5 is not an issue). Here are the findings:

### **ISSUE #1: `endBattle(LOSE)` does not remove `scoreUpdateTimer`**
**Severity**: Medium  
**Location**: `BattleGameScene.ts`, lines 219, 306-332

When the player loses (hits a poop), `endBattle(BattleResult.LOSE)` is called, but the code only removes the timer when `result === BattleResult.WIN || result === BattleResult.DISCONNECT` (line 317-319). On LOSE, `gameOver` is set to true (by parent `hitPoop()` before the call), so the timer's callback guard `if (!this.gameOver)` prevents firing, but the timer object still exists in memory and continues to fire the callback 1000ms apart.

**Fix**: Remove the timer unconditionally for all result types:
```typescript
private endBattle(result: BattleResult) {
  if (this.battleFinished) return;
  this.battleFinished = true;
  
  if (this.disconnectTimer) {
    clearTimeout(this.disconnectTimer);
    this.disconnectTimer = null;
  }
  
  this.hideDisconnectWarning();
  
  // Remove scoreUpdateTimer for ALL results, not just WIN/DISCONNECT
  if (this.scoreUpdateTimer) this.scoreUpdateTimer.remove();
  
  if (result === BattleResult.WIN || result === BattleResult.DISCONNECT) {
    this.gameOver = true;
    this.physics.pause();
    this.sound.stopAll();
  }
  // ... rest of method
}
```

---

### **ISSUE #2: `shutdown()` in BattleGameScene calls `destroy()` fire-and-forget (async without await)**
**Severity**: High  
**Location**: `BattleGameScene.ts`, lines 341-343

The `shutdown()` method calls `this.battleChannel.destroy()` synchronously but does not await it. Since `destroy()` is async (lines 157-162 in `battleChannel.ts`), the untrack operation may not complete before the scene is torn down. The channel could persist in Supabase Realtime's subscription state or leave orphaned WebSocket listeners.

**Real scenario**: If the player quickly navigates away (e.g., presses back, encounters an error), the scene destroys but the channel unsubscription is still pending, potentially leaving the player "in the room" for a few seconds on the opponent's end.

**Fix**: Make `shutdown()` async and await the destroy:
```typescript
async shutdown() {
  if (this.scoreUpdateTimer) this.scoreUpdateTimer.remove();
  if (this.disconnectTimer) {
    clearTimeout(this.disconnectTimer);
    this.disconnectTimer = null;
  }
  if (this.battleChannel) {
    await this.battleChannel.destroy();
    this.battleChannel = null;
  }
}
```

However, this requires Phaser to support async shutdown. If Phaser doesn't, use `destroyImmediate()` instead (like BattleMatchScene does on line 412):
```typescript
shutdown() {
  if (this.scoreUpdateTimer) this.scoreUpdateTimer.remove();
  if (this.disconnectTimer) {
    clearTimeout(this.disconnectTimer);
    this.disconnectTimer = null;
  }
  if (this.battleChannel) {
    this.battleChannel.destroyImmediate();
    this.battleChannel = null;
  }
}
```

---

### **ISSUE #3: Initial `sendScoreUpdate(0)` is a no-op that wastes network traffic**
**Severity**: Low  
**Location**: `BattleGameScene.ts`, line 117

Right after `this.battleChannel.subscribe()` completes, the code sends `sendScoreUpdate(0)`. At game start, the score is always 0 (see line 40: `this.opponentScore = 0`), so this is unnecessary network traffic. The opponent's score will update on their end as soon as you begin scoring.

**Fix**: Remove this line entirely. The periodic timer (line 106-114) already handles score synchronization:
```typescript
// Delete line 117: this.battleChannel.sendScoreUpdate(this.score);
```

---

### **ISSUE #4: Potential memory leak in `onReceivePoop()` — `delayedCall` callback holds closure to `poop` object**
**Severity**: Medium  
**Location**: `BattleGameScene.ts`, lines 234-254

In `onReceivePoop()`, when receiving opponent's attack poops, the code creates a delayed callback that references `poop`:
```typescript
this.time.delayedCall(300, () => {
  if (poop.active) poop.clearTint();
});
```

If the scene is destroyed **before the 300ms callback fires**, the callback still holds a reference to the poop object and will attempt to call `poop.clearTint()` after the scene is destroyed. This doesn't cause a hard crash (due to the `if (poop.active)` guard), but it's a dangling callback that fires unnecessarily.

Similarly, in `showReceiveWarning()` (lines 262-282), tweens are created with `onComplete` callbacks that destroy GameObjects. If the scene shuts down mid-tween, the callback will still fire and try to destroy objects in a dead scene.

**Fix**: Track these timers and tweens in instance properties and cancel them on shutdown:
```typescript
private receivePoopTimers: Phaser.Time.TimerEvent[] = [];

private onReceivePoop(count: number) {
  // ... existing code ...
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    // ...
    const timer = this.time.delayedCall(300, () => {
      if (poop.active) poop.clearTint();
    });
    this.receivePoopTimers.push(timer);
  }
}

shutdown() {
  // ... existing cleanup ...
  for (const timer of this.receivePoopTimers) {
    timer.remove();
  }
  this.receivePoopTimers = [];
}
```

For tweens, Phaser automatically cleans them up on scene shutdown, but for defensive coding, you could explicitly stop them:
```typescript
shutdown() {
  this.tweens.killAll();
  // ... other cleanup ...
}
```

---

### **NOT AN ISSUE: `BattleMatchScene.shutdown()` doesn't destroy channel**

This is **intentional and correct**. The `goToBattle()` method (line 404-417) calls `destroyImmediate()` **before** scene transition, which properly removes the channel from Supabase's singleton registry. If the scene is stopped unexpectedly (non-goToBattle path), the channel is simply left in memory but will be garbage-collected when BattleMatchScene is destroyed, and Supabase will clean up the WebSocket on its side after the presence times out (~30s).

The `goBack()` method (line 426-434) properly awaits `destroy()`, so manual cleanup is correct there.

---

## Summary

| Issue | Severity | Fix |
|-------|----------|-----|
| 1. LOSE doesn't remove scoreUpdateTimer | Medium | Remove timer unconditionally in `endBattle()` |
| 2. shutdown() doesn't await destroy() | High | Use `destroyImmediate()` or make shutdown async |
| 3. Initial sendScoreUpdate(0) | Low | Delete the redundant call on line 117 |
| 4. onReceivePoop() timers leak | Medium | Track timers in instance array, cancel on shutdown |

  "old_string": "  async create() {\n    super.create();\n\n    // 배경 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    const bg = this.add.image(200, 300, 'background2');\n    bg.setDisplaySize(400, 600);\n    this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0.6);\n\n    // 타이틀 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    this.add.text(200, 60, '⚔️ 대전 모드', {\n      fontSize: '32px',\n      color: '#ff4444',\n      fontStyle: 'bold',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 5,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    this.add.text(200, 105, 'EXTREME 난이도로 1대1 대전!', {\n      fontSize: '14px',\n      color: '#cccccc',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 3,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    // 뒤로가기 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    const backBtn = this.add.text(200, 560, '← 메인 메뉴', {\n      fontSize: '18px',\n      color: '#aaaaaa',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 3,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n    backBtn.on('pointerover', () => backBtn.setColor('#ffffff'));\n    backBtn.on('pointerout', () => backBtn.setColor('#aaaaaa'));\n    backBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.goBack());\n\n    // 유저 ID\n    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n    this.userId = data.user?.id ?? `anon-${Date.now()}`;\n\n    // 초기 상태: 메뉴\n    this.showMenu();\n  }",
  "new_string": "  create() {\n    super.create();\n\n    // 배경 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    const bg = this.add.image(200, 300, 'background2');\n    bg.setDisplaySize(400, 600);\n    this.add.rectangle(200, 300, 400, 600, 0x000000, 0.6);\n\n    // 타이틀 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    this.add.text(200, 60, '⚔️ 대전 모드', {\n      fontSize: '32px',\n      color: '#ff4444',\n      fontStyle: 'bold',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 5,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    this.add.text(200, 105, 'EXTREME 난이도로 1대1 대전!', {\n      fontSize: '14px',\n      color: '#cccccc',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 3,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5);\n\n    // 뒤로가기 (모든 상태에서 유지)\n    const backBtn = this.add.text(200, 560, '← 메인 메뉴', {\n      fontSize: '18px',\n      color: '#aaaaaa',\n      stroke: '#000',\n      strokeThickness: 3,\n    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });\n    backBtn.on('pointerover', () => backBtn.setColor('#ffffff'));\n    backBtn.on('pointerout', () => backBtn.setColor('#aaaaaa'));\n    backBtn.on('pointerdown', () => this.goBack());\n\n    // 유저 ID: 즉시 임시값 사용 → 백그라운드에서 실제 ID로 교체\n    // (Phaser는 async create()를 await하지 않으므로 동기 처리)\n    this.userId = `anon-${Date.now()}`;\n    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {\n      if (data.user?.id) this.userId = data.user.id;\n    });\n\n    // 초기 상태: 메뉴\n    this.showMenu();\n  }"
}

     5→현재 게임에는 솔로 플레이(클래식 모드)만 존재한다. 친구끼리 경쟁할 수 있는 **1대1 실시간 대전 모드**를 추가하여 게임의 리플레이 가치를 높인다. Supabase Realtime Broadcast를 활용해 각자 자기 화면에서 EXTREME 난이도로 플레이하며, 특수 똥 수집 시 상대 화면에 일반 똥을 보내는 방해 메커니즘을 구현한다.
     6→
     7→## 핵심 설계 결정

    10→- **승패**: 상대가 먼저 죽으면 즉시 승리
    11→- **상대 점수**: 게임 중 실시간 표시
    12→- **난이도**: EXTREME 고정
    13→- **능력**: 캐릭터 능력 정상 작동
    14→- **방해 메커니즘**: 특수 똥(금/다이아/토파즈/무지개) 수집 → 상대에게 일반 똥 1개 전송

    27→
    28→Presence로 상대 접속/이탈 감지 (연결 끊김 → 5초 대기 → 남은 쪽 승리).

Perfect! I have all the information I need. Let me now provide you with a comprehensive report of the codebase structure.

## Comprehensive Codebase Exploration Report

### 1. **Leaderboard System Overview**

The leaderboard system is **Supabase-based** with a database schema including `leaderboard` and `profiles` tables. Here's how it works:

#### Client-side (`src/utils/leaderboard.ts`)
- **Main functions**:
  - `submitScore()` — Submits score to `leaderboard-submit` Edge Function
  - `getLeaderboard()` — Fetches rankings from `leaderboard-top` Edge Function
  - `startGameSession()` — Creates a game session via `game-start` Edge Function for anticheat
  - `getUserId()`, `getUserInitials()`, `setUserInitials()` — Manages 3-character initials in localStorage
  - `hasUserInitials()` — Checks if initials are set

#### Server-side (`supabase/functions/`)
**Three Edge Functions handle leaderboard operations**:

---

### 2. **Edge Function: leaderboard-submit** 
Path: `/supabase/functions/leaderboard-submit/index.ts`

**Purpose**: Submit game score to leaderboard with comprehensive anticheat

**Key validations**:
- **Hard score cap** by difficulty (easy: 10k, normal: 15k, hard: 20k, extreme/physical: 30k)
- **JWT authentication** — Requires valid Supabase Auth token in Authorization header
- **Session-based verification** — Validates game session created server-side:
  - Atomically updates `game_sessions.used = true` while validating ownership/difficulty/freshness
  - Enforces 2-hour session expiry
  - **Time-based validation**: Calculates expected score from elapsed time accounting for bonus items:
    ```
    bonusScore = gold*20 + diamond*40 + topaz*80 + rainbow*90
    timeBasedScore = score - bonusScore
    minRequiredSec = timeBasedScore * 0.1 * 0.5  (50% tolerance)
    ```
  - Rejects scores achieved faster than physically possible

**Database operations**:
1. Updates `profiles.initials` with user's 3-letter initials
2. Upserts into `leaderboard` table (keeps only highest score per user/difficulty)
3. Calculates rank by counting entries with higher scores

**Response**: `SubmitScoreResponse` with:
- `success: boolean`
- `isNewRecord: boolean`
- `previousScore: number | null`
- `newScore: number`
- `rank: number | null`

---

### 3. **Edge Function: leaderboard-top**
Path: `/supabase/functions/leaderboard-top/index.ts`

**Purpose**: Fetch public leaderboard rankings

**Key features**:
- **Optional authentication** — Works with or without JWT (public rankings)
- **Optional current user rank** — If authenticated, returns requesting user's rank/score
- **profiles JOIN** — Fetches initials from profiles table alongside scores

**Database query**:
- Queries `leaderboard` table JOINed with `profiles` on `initials`
- Orders by `score DESC`
- Returns top N entries (default 100, max 100)
- If currentUserId provided, counts how many scores are higher to calculate rank

**Response**: `LeaderboardResponse` with:
- `success: boolean`
- `difficulty: string`
- `leaderboard: LeaderboardEntry[]` (userId, userName, score, rank, characterType)
- `currentUserRank: { rank, score } | null`
- `totalEntries: number`

---

### 4. **Edge Function: game-start**
Path: `/supabase/functions/game-start/index.ts`

**Purpose**: Create server-stamped game session for anticheat

**Key mechanism**:
- Requires JWT authentication
- Creates `game_sessions` record with:
  - `user_id`: Current authenticated user
  - `difficulty`: Game difficulty
  - `start_time`: **Server timestamp** (client time not trusted)
  - `used: false` (consumed by leaderboard-submit atomically)

**Response**: `{ sessionId: string }`

---

### 5. **Auth System**
Path: `src/utils/auth.ts`

**Simple, Supabase-native anonymous auth**:
- `ensureLoggedIn()` — Auto-signs in anonymously if not logged in
- `getCurrentUserId()` — Returns authenticated user's UUID
- `getCurrentUser()` — Returns full user object or null

**Design philosophy**: Anonymous auth allows unauthenticated gameplay while enabling score submissions and SKOR tracking per device.

---

### 6. **SKOR System**
Path: `src/utils/skor.ts`

**Client implementation**:
- `submitSkor(data)` — Posts game result data to `skor-submit` Edge Function
  - Data includes: score, goldCollected, diamondCollected, topazCollected, rainbowCollected
  - Handles token refresh: calls `getSession()` which auto-refreshes expired tokens
  - Falls back to anonymous login if no session
  - Explicitly injects `Authorization` header (defensive against SDK auto-injection lapses)
- `getSkorBalance()` — Queries `user_skor` table, initializes new users with 10,000 SKOR welcome bonus
- `getCachedSkorBalance()` / `cacheSkorBalance()` — localStorage cache for instant UI updates

---

### 7. **Edge Function: skor-submit**
Path: `/supabase/functions/skor-submit/index.ts`

**Purpose**: Calculate and distribute SKOR rewards based on game performance

**SKOR calculation**:
```
Game SKOR = min(
  (gold*0.5 + diamond*1.5 + topaz*3.5 + rainbow*10.0),
  bracketCap(score)
)
```

Where `bracketCap` increases with score:
- score < 1000: 40 SKOR max
- score < 2000: 70 SKOR max
- score < 3000: 90 SKOR max
- score >= 3000: 110 SKOR max

**Quest progression** (cumulative item collection milestones):
```javascript
QUESTS = [
  { key: 'gold',    interval: 300,  reward: 5  },  // Every 300 golds
  { key: 'diamond', interval: 150,  reward: 8  },
  { key: 'topaz',   interval: 80,   reward: 10 },
  { key: 'rainbow', interval: 50,   reward: 15 },
]
```

**Database updates**:
1. Upserts `user_skor` (balance, weekly_earned, week_start)
2. Upserts `quest_progress` (gold_total, diamond_total, topaz_total, rainbow_total)

**Response**: `SkorSubmitResponse` with:
- `skorEarned: number` (game SKOR only)
- `bracketCap: number`
- `questRewards: { quest, reward }[]`
- `totalSkorAdded: number` (game + quests)
- `remainingBalance: number`
- `weeklyCapRemaining: -1` (no weekly limit)

---

### 8. **LeaderboardScene** (`src/scenes/LeaderboardScene.ts`)

**UI/UX**:
- Displays top 10 rankings per difficulty
- 4 difficulty buttons (NORMAL, HARD, EXTREME, PHYSICAL)
- Loads leaderboard asynchronously with "로딩 중..." placeholder
- Shows current user's rank if authenticated
- Rank-based coloring: 1st=gold, 2nd=silver, 3rd=bronze
- Back button returns to ModeSelectScene

**Request deduplication**: Uses `currentRequestId` to ignore stale responses when user rapidly switches difficulties

---

### 9. **Database Schema Summary**

Based on the Edge Functions, the Supabase schema includes:

| Table | Key Columns | Purpose |
|-------|-------------|---------|
| `leaderboard` | user_id, difficulty, score, character_type, updated_at | High scores per user/difficulty |
| `profiles` | id (user_id), initials | User's 3-letter display name |
| `game_sessions` | id, user_id, difficulty, start_time, used | Anticheat session tokens |
| `user_skor` | user_id, balance, weekly_earned, week_start, updated_at | SKOR balance tracking |
| `quest_progress` | user_id, gold_total, diamond_total, topaz_total, rainbow_total | Cumulative item collection |

---

### 10. **Key Flow Diagrams**

**Score Submission Flow**:
```
GameScene.gameOver()
  ↓
startGameSession(difficulty) → game-start Edge Function
  ↓ returns sessionId
submitScore(score, difficulty, initials, sessionId, verification)
  ↓
leaderboard-submit Edge Function
  ├─ Validates JWT
  ├─ Verifies sessionId atomically
  ├─ Time-gates score (50% tolerance)
  ├─ Upserts leaderboard record
  └─ Returns rank & new record status
```

**SKOR Submission Flow**:
```
GameScene.gameOver()
  ↓
submitSkor({ score, goldCollected, ... })
  ↓
skor-submit Edge Function
  ├─ Validates JWT
  ├─ Calculates game SKOR (capped by bracket)
  ├─ Updates quest_progress & checks milestones
  ├─ Adds to user_skor balance
  └─ Returns SKOR earned & quest bonuses
```

**Leaderboard Display Flow**:
```
LeaderboardScene.selectDifficulty()
  ↓
getLeaderboard(difficulty, limit=10)
  ↓
leaderboard-top Edge Function
  ├─ Queries leaderboard JOIN profiles
  ├─ Calculates current user rank (if authenticated)
  └─ Returns sorted entries
  ↓
displayLeaderboard() — Renders UI
```

---

### 11. **Important Implementation Details**

1. **Anticheat Strategy**:
   - Game sessions created server-side (client time untrusted)
   - Atomically consumed (prevents replay)
   - Time-gated validation (50% tolerance for bonus items)
   - Hard score caps prevent impossible submissions

2. **SKOR Inflation Control**:
   - Bracket caps limit game SKOR per session (max 110)
   - Quest milestones are cumulative (can only trigger once per threshold)
   - No weekly caps currently (field returns -1)

3. **Initials Management**:
   - 3-character uppercase validation: `/^[A-Z]{3}$/`
   - Stored in localStorage AND profiles table
   - Updated on each new score submission

4. **Session Token Pattern**:
   - Used for both leaderboard and SKOR submissions
   - Prevents client clock tampering
   - One-time use (atomically marked `used=true`)

All files are located at the absolute paths shown in this report. The system is production-ready with comprehensive anticheat and validation mechanisms.

### 1. **BattleResultScene** (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/scenes/BattleResultScene.ts`)

**Full contents** (132 lines):
- Displays battle results (WIN/LOSE/DISCONNECT) with winner/loser titles and colors
- Shows both players' final scores in a VS comparison layout
- Two buttons: "⚔️ 재대전" (Rematch → BattleMatchScene) and "메인 메뉴" (Main → ModeSelectScene)
- Receives data via `init(data: BattleResultData)`:
  - `result: BattleResult` (WIN | LOSE | DISCONNECT)
  - `myScore: number`
  - `opponentScore: number`
  - `roomCode: string` (not currently used in display)
- Disconnects don't persist any battle records to database

---

### 2. **BattleGameScene** (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/src/scenes/BattleGameScene.ts`)

**Key flow**:
1. Extends `GameScene` and forces **EXTREME difficulty + BATTLE mode**
2. **Channel lifecycle**: 
   - `initBattleChannel()` creates new channel → registers listeners → subscribes → starts score timer
   - Real-time Supabase Realtime Broadcast events exchanged
3. **Special poop collection** → sends 1 poop to opponent via `battleChannel.sendPoop()`
4. **Game over handling**:
   - `hitPoop()` triggers `endBattle(BattleResult.LOSE)` after parent hit detection
   - Opponent game over triggers `endBattle(BattleResult.WIN)` via broadcast listener
   - Opponent disconnect: 5-second warning, then `endBattle(BattleResult.DISCONNECT)` (auto-win)
5. **endBattle() flow** (line 303-331):
   - Sets `battleFinished = true`
   - Cleans up timers and channels
   - **Does NOT submit scores to leaderboard** (overrides `showGameOverUI()` with empty implementation)
   - After 1 second delay: `scene.start('BattleResultScene', { result, myScore, opponentScore, roomCode })`
6. **shutdown()**: Synchronously destroys battleChannel to prevent message leaks after scene switch

CREATE TABLE game_sessions (

  user_id     UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  difficulty  TEXT        NOT NULL,

  used        BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

);
```
- **Purpose**: Anti-cheat mechanism. Server records session start time.
- **Used by**: `leaderboard-submit` edge function validates score timing against this.

);

  gold_total   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

);

CREATE TABLE leaderboard (

);
```

### 4. **Leaderboard Edge Functions**

#### **leaderboard-submit** (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/leaderboard-submit/index.ts`)
- **Route**: Called via `supabase.functions.invoke('leaderboard-submit')`
- **Input**:
  ```typescript
  {
    score: number,
    difficulty: 'easy' | 'normal' | 'hard' | 'extreme' | 'physical',
    userName: string,        // 3-char initials (validated)
    sessionId: string | null, // From game-start function
    verification: { goldCollected, diamondCollected, topazCollected, rainbowCollected },
    characterType: string    // Character used in game
  }
  ```
- **Validation chain**:
  1. Score range check (hard caps: easy=10k, normal=15k, hard=20k, extreme/physical=30k)
  2. JWT authentication required
  3. **Session check**: `UPDATE ... WHERE used=false AND user_id=? AND difficulty=?` (atomic consume)
     - If sessionId provided: validates elapsed time ≥ required minimum for score
     - Otherwise: uses legacy verification data with 20% tolerance
  4. Updates `profiles.initials` with userName
  5. **Upsert logic**: Only saves if score > existing score for (user_id, difficulty)
  6. Calculates rank via `ZREVRANK`
- **Returns**:
  ```typescript
  {
    success: true,
    isNewRecord: boolean,
    previousScore: number | null,
    newScore: number,
    rank: number | null
  }
  ```
- **Called by**: `GameScene` when `isNewRecord === true` after user enters initials
- **NOT called by**: `BattleGameScene` (no leaderboard integration in battles)

#### **leaderboard-top** (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/leaderboard-top/index.ts`)
- **Route**: Called via `supabase.functions.invoke('leaderboard-top')`
- **Input**:
  ```typescript
  {
    difficulty: 'easy' | 'normal' | 'hard' | 'extreme' | 'physical',
    limit?: number (default 100, max 100)
  }
  ```
- **Auth**: Optional JWT (allows anonymous leaderboard viewing)
- **Query**:
  1. `SELECT leaderboard JOIN profiles WHERE difficulty = ?` → top N entries ordered by score DESC
  2. If JWT provided: fetches current user's rank/score separately
- **Returns**:
  ```typescript
  {
    success: true,
    difficulty: string,
    leaderboard: Array<{ userId, userName, score, rank, characterType }>,
    currentUserRank: { rank, score } | null,
    totalEntries: number
  }
  ```

#### **game-start** (`/Users/johyunduk/Projects/ddong-avoid-game/supabase/functions/game-start/index.ts`)
- **Route**: Called via `supabase.functions.invoke('game-start')`
- **Input**: `{ difficulty }`
- **Auth**: Requires JWT
- **Operation**: Creates new `game_sessions` row with server `now()` as `start_time`
- **Returns**: `{ sessionId: string }` (UUID)
- **Purpose**: Anti-cheat — server records when session started; client can't manipulate time

### 5. **Current Battle Architecture Limitations**

**NO Supabase integration for battles**:
- Battle results (WIN/LOSE/DISCONNECT) are **displayed only**, not persisted
- No `battle_records` or `battle_stats` table exists
- No points/rewards awarded to winner
- No battle history tracking
- `BattleResultScene` passes `roomCode` to `init()` but never uses it

**Realtime communication**:
- Uses Supabase Realtime Broadcast for live score updates
- Events: READY, GAME_START, SEND_POOP, SCORE_UPDATE, GAME_OVER
- Presence detection for disconnect warnings
- **No persistence of these events** — battle is ephemeral

## Battle Mode Ranking System — Implementation Plan

### Overview of Execution Order

The work breaks down into 5 sequential layers: DB migration → Edge Functions → Client util → BattleLeaderboardScene → modify existing scenes. Each layer depends on the previous one.

---

## Step 1: Database Migration

**File: `supabase/migrations/20260321_battle_records.sql`**

```sql
-- 배틀 전적 테이블
-- PRIMARY KEY = user_id (사용자당 1행, 카운터만 증가)
CREATE TABLE public.battle_records (
  user_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  wins         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  losses       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  disconnects  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- 부전승 (상대 먹튀)
  updated_at   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

-- 랭킹 정렬용 인덱스:
-- (wins + disconnects) DESC, win_rate DESC 순
-- win_rate = wins+disconnects / NULLIF(wins+losses+disconnects, 0)
-- 복합 계산이라 함수 기반 인덱스 사용
CREATE INDEX idx_battle_records_rank
  ON public.battle_records ((wins + disconnects) DESC, updated_at DESC);

-- RLS
ALTER TABLE public.battle_records ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 읽기: 전체 공개 (랭킹 조회용)
CREATE POLICY "battle_records_select_public"
  ON public.battle_records FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- 쓰기: Edge Function (service_role)만 허용
-- 클라이언트 직접 쓰기 금지 (치팅 방지)
-- INSERT/UPDATE 정책 없음 → service_role key로만 가능
```

**설계 근거:**
- 단일 행 upsert 패턴은 `leaderboard` 테이블의 `(user_id, difficulty)` 패턴과 동일
- `wins + disconnects`를 컬럼으로 저장하지 않고 쿼리 시 계산 — 중복 데이터 없음
- RLS에서 SELECT는 공개(랭킹 조회), INSERT/UPDATE는 정책 없음으로 service_role만 가능

---

## Step 2: Edge Function — `battle-result-submit`

**Directory: `supabase/functions/battle-result-submit/index.ts`**

```typescript
import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
};

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    const { result } = await req.json();

    // 입력 검증
    const validResults = ['win', 'lose', 'disconnect'];
    if (!validResults.includes(result)) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid result: must be win, lose, or disconnect' }),
        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      );
    }

    // JWT 인증 필수 (leaderboard-submit과 동일 패턴)
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    if (!authHeader) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing authorization header' }),
        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      );
    }

    const supabaseUser = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    );

    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
    if (authError || !user) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }),
        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      );
    }

    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? ''
    );

    // 원자적 upsert: 기존 행이 없으면 INSERT, 있으면 UPDATE (카운터 증가)
    // PostgreSQL의 ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE로 race condition 방지
    // Supabase JS SDK에는 atomic increment RPC 또는 raw SQL 사용
    const incrementColumn = result === 'win' ? 'wins'
      : result === 'lose' ? 'losses'
      : 'disconnects';

    // rpc 방식: DB 함수로 원자적 upsert (아래 migration에 함수 추가)
    const { data: record, error: rpcError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .rpc('increment_battle_record', {
        p_user_id: user.id,
        p_column: incrementColumn,
      });

    if (rpcError) {
      console.error('RPC error:', rpcError);
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to update battle record' }),
        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      );
    }

    // record = { wins, losses, disconnects, updated_at }
    const { wins, losses, disconnects } = record;
    const totalWins = wins + disconnects;
    const totalGames = wins + losses + disconnects;
    const winRate = totalGames > 0 ? Math.round((totalWins / totalGames) * 1000) / 10 : 0;

    // 현재 유저의 랭킹 계산
    // "나보다 (wins+disconnects)가 높은 사람 수 + 1"
    const { count: higherCount } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('battle_records')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .gt('wins', -1) // 전체 행 대상
      // 복합 조건: wins + disconnects > totalWins
      // Supabase JS는 computed column 필터 불가 → filter() 사용
      .filter('(wins::int + disconnects::int)', 'gt', totalWins);
    // 주의: Supabase JS SDK로 "wins + disconnects > N" 조건은 직접 지원 안 됨
    // → RPC 함수에서 rank도 함께 반환하는 방식으로 처리 (아래 설명)

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        success: true,
        wins,
        losses,
        disconnects,
        winRate,
        rank: (higherCount ?? 0) + 1,
      }),
      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: 'Internal server error' }),
      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }
});
```

**중요: RPC 함수를 migration에 추가해야 함**

`wins + disconnects > N` 필터는 Supabase JS SDK로 직접 표현이 불가능하므로, 마이그레이션에 PostgreSQL 함수를 함께 추가한다.

**Migration에 추가할 DB 함수:**

```sql
-- battle_records 원자적 upsert + rank 반환 함수
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION increment_battle_record(
  p_user_id  UUID,
  p_column   TEXT  -- 'wins' | 'losses' | 'disconnects'
)
RETURNS TABLE(
  wins        INTEGER,
  losses      INTEGER,
  disconnects INTEGER,
  rank        BIGINT
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER  -- service_role 없이 호출해도 권한 승격
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_wins        INTEGER;
  v_losses      INTEGER;
  v_disconnects INTEGER;
  v_total_wins  INTEGER;
  v_rank        BIGINT;
BEGIN
  -- 원자적 upsert: INSERT 없으면 0으로 초기화, 있으면 해당 컬럼만 +1
  INSERT INTO public.battle_records (user_id, wins, losses, disconnects, updated_at)
  VALUES (p_user_id, 0, 0, 0, NOW())
  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO NOTHING;

  -- 해당 컬럼만 +1 (동적 SQL 사용)
  EXECUTE format(
    'UPDATE public.battle_records SET %I = %I + 1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE user_id = $1',
    p_column, p_column
  ) USING p_user_id;

  -- 최신 행 조회
  SELECT br.wins, br.losses, br.disconnects
  INTO v_wins, v_losses, v_disconnects
  FROM public.battle_records br
  WHERE br.user_id = p_user_id;

  v_total_wins := v_wins + v_disconnects;

  -- 랭킹: 나보다 (wins + disconnects)가 높은 사람 수 + 1
  SELECT COUNT(*) + 1
  INTO v_rank
  FROM public.battle_records br
  WHERE (br.wins + br.disconnects) > v_total_wins;

  RETURN QUERY SELECT v_wins, v_losses, v_disconnects, v_rank;
END;
$$;

-- 보안: 함수 호출 권한은 service_role만 (SECURITY DEFINER이므로 anon에서 직접 호출 불가)
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION increment_battle_record FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION increment_battle_record TO service_role;
```

**이 패턴의 이점:**
- 단일 DB 왕복으로 upsert + increment + rank 계산을 원자적으로 처리
- race condition 없음 (UPDATE는 row-level lock)
- RPC 결과를 직접 Edge Function 응답에 매핑

**배포 명령:**
```bash
supabase functions deploy battle-result-submit --no-verify-jwt
```
JWT는 함수 내부에서 직접 검증하므로 `--no-verify-jwt` 필요 (leaderboard-submit과 동일).

---

## Step 3: Edge Function — `battle-leaderboard-top`

**Directory: `supabase/functions/battle-leaderboard-top/index.ts`**

```typescript
import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
};

Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(1, parseInt(body.limit ?? '20', 10)), 100);

    // 선택적 JWT (leaderboard-top과 동일 패턴: 비로그인도 허용)
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    let currentUserId: string | null = null;

    if (authHeader) {
      const supabaseUser = createClient(
        Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
        Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
        { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
      );
      const { data: { user } } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
      currentUserId = user?.id ?? null;
    }

    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? ''
    );

    // 리더보드 조회: battle_records JOIN profiles
    // 정렬: (wins + disconnects) DESC → win_rate DESC (동점 처리)
    // win_rate = (wins + disconnects) / NULLIF(wins + losses + disconnects, 0)
    const { data: leaderboard, error: lbError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('battle_records')
      .select(`
        user_id,
        wins,
        losses,
        disconnects,
        profiles!inner(initials)
      `)
      .order('wins', { ascending: false })      // 근사치; 정확한 정렬은 아래 설명
      .limit(limit);
    // 주의: wins+disconnects DESC 복합 정렬은 Supabase JS SDK로 직접 불가
    // → DB VIEW 또는 RPC 사용 (아래 설명)
```

**정렬 문제 해결:** `wins + disconnects` 복합 정렬은 Supabase JS SDK의 `.order()` 로는 표현 불가. 두 가지 해결책 중 **DB View** 방식이 더 깔끔하다.

**Migration에 추가할 View:**

```sql
-- 배틀 랭킹 뷰 (정렬 포함)
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.battle_leaderboard_view AS
SELECT
  br.user_id,
  br.wins,
  br.losses,
  br.disconnects,
  (br.wins + br.disconnects) AS total_wins,
  CASE
    WHEN (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects) = 0 THEN 0.0
    ELSE ROUND(
      (br.wins + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC /
      (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC * 100,
      1
    )
  END AS win_rate,
  p.initials
FROM public.battle_records br
JOIN public.profiles p ON p.id = br.user_id
ORDER BY (br.wins + br.disconnects) DESC,
         CASE WHEN (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects) = 0 THEN 0.0
              ELSE (br.wins + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC /
                   (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC
         END DESC;

-- View에 RLS 적용 불가 → 읽기는 서비스롤만 사용 (Edge Function 내부)
-- 또는 anon에게 SELECT 권한 부여 (랭킹은 공개 데이터이므로 허용)
GRANT SELECT ON public.battle_leaderboard_view TO anon, authenticated;
```

**Edge Function 이어서:**

```typescript
    // View에서 조회 (정렬 포함)
    const { data: leaderboard, error: lbError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('battle_leaderboard_view')
      .select('*')
      .limit(limit);

    if (lbError) {
      console.error('Leaderboard query error:', lbError);
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to fetch battle leaderboard' }),
        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      );
    }

    const entries = (leaderboard ?? []).map((entry, index) => ({
      rank: index + 1,
      userId: entry.user_id,
      userName: entry.initials ?? '???',
      wins: entry.wins,
      losses: entry.losses,
      disconnects: entry.disconnects,
      totalWins: entry.total_wins,
      winRate: entry.win_rate,
    }));

    // 현재 유저 순위
    let currentUserRank: {
      rank: number; wins: number; losses: number;
      disconnects: number; winRate: number;
    } | null = null;

    if (currentUserId) {
      // View에서 현재 유저 위치 계산: total_wins 기준 상위 카운트 + 1
      const { data: userRecord } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from('battle_records')
        .select('wins, losses, disconnects')
        .eq('user_id', currentUserId)
        .single();

      if (userRecord) {
        const userTotalWins = userRecord.wins + userRecord.disconnects;
        // 나보다 total_wins 높은 사람 수 (View에서 직접 RPC로 계산)
        const { count: higherCount } = await supabaseAdmin
          .rpc('get_battle_rank', { p_total_wins: userTotalWins });

        const totalGames = userRecord.wins + userRecord.losses + userRecord.disconnects;
        currentUserRank = {
          rank: (higherCount ?? 0) + 1,
          wins: userRecord.wins,
          losses: userRecord.losses,
          disconnects: userRecord.disconnects,
          winRate: totalGames > 0
            ? Math.round((userTotalWins / totalGames) * 1000) / 10
            : 0,
        };
      }
    }

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        success: true,
        leaderboard: entries,
        currentUserRank,
      }),
      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: 'Internal server error' }),
      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }
});
```

**Migration에 추가할 rank 계산 함수:**

```sql
-- 주어진 total_wins보다 높은 사람 수 반환
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_battle_rank(p_total_wins INTEGER)
RETURNS BIGINT
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
  SELECT COUNT(*)
  FROM public.battle_records
  WHERE (wins + disconnects) > p_total_wins;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION get_battle_rank TO anon, authenticated, service_role;
```

**배포 명령:**
```bash
supabase functions deploy battle-leaderboard-top --no-verify-jwt
```

## Step 4: Client Util — `src/utils/battleLeaderboard.ts`

`skor.ts`의 세션 갱신 패턴을 그대로 따른다.

```typescript
import { supabase } from './supabase';
import { type BattleResult } from '../types/BattleTypes';

export interface BattleRecordEntry {
  rank: number;
  userId: string;
  userName: string;
  wins: number;
  losses: number;
  disconnects: number;
  totalWins: number;
  winRate: number;
}

export interface SubmitBattleResultResponse {
  success: boolean;
  wins: number;
  losses: number;
  disconnects: number;
  winRate: number;
  rank: number;
}

export interface BattleLeaderboardResponse {
  success: boolean;
  leaderboard: BattleRecordEntry[];
  currentUserRank: {
    rank: number;
    wins: number;
    losses: number;
    disconnects: number;
    winRate: number;
  } | null;
}

/**
 * 대전 결과 제출 (skor.ts와 동일한 세션 갱신 패턴)
 */
export async function submitBattleResult(
  result: BattleResult
): Promise<SubmitBattleResultResponse> {
  // getSession()으로 토큰 자동 갱신 (skor.ts 패턴)
  let { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

  if (!session) {
    const { data: authData } = await supabase.auth.signInAnonymously();
    session = authData.session;
  }

  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('battle-result-submit', {
    body: { result },
    headers: session?.access_token
      ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}` }
      : undefined,
  });

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message || 'Failed to submit battle result');
  }

  return data as SubmitBattleResultResponse;
}

/**
 * 배틀 리더보드 조회 (leaderboard.ts의 getLeaderboard와 동일 패턴)
 */
export async function getBattleLeaderboard(
  limit: number = 20
): Promise<BattleLeaderboardResponse> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('battle-leaderboard-top', {
    body: { limit },
  });

  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message || 'Failed to fetch battle leaderboard');
  }

  return data as BattleLeaderboardResponse;
}
```

**함수 시그니처 요약:**
- `submitBattleResult(result: BattleResult): Promise<SubmitBattleResultResponse>`
- `getBattleLeaderboard(limit?: number): Promise<BattleLeaderboardResponse>`

`LeaderboardScene.ts`를 직접 참조 모델로 삼아 단일뷰(탭 없음)로 구성.

**UI 레이아웃 (400x600 캔버스):**

```

y=90   내 전적: W승 L패 (D부전승) | 승률: XX%  (현재 유저)

## TypeScript 준수 체크리스트

모든 신규/수정 파일에서 확인:

| 규칙 | 체크 |
|------|------|
| `BattleResult` 타입 import는 `import { type BattleResult }` | `BattleResultScene.ts`, `battleLeaderboard.ts` |
| `BattleRecordEntry` 등 인터페이스 import도 `type` 키워드 | `BattleLeaderboardScene.ts` |
| enum 없음 — BattleResult는 이미 `as const` | 기존 준수 |
| `LeaderboardEntry` 등 외부 타입 import 시 `type` | `BattleLeaderboardScene.ts` |

`npx tsc --noEmit` 실행으로 최종 확인.

---

## 잠재적 문제 및 주의사항

**1. 중복 제출 방지**
`BattleResultScene`에서 `create()`가 두 번 호출되는 경우(씬 재시작)를 대비해 플래그 변수 `private resultSubmitted = false`를 추가하고 `init()`에서 초기화, `submitBattleResult` 호출 전 체크.

**2. 연결 끊김 시 양쪽 모두 disconnect 제출 문제**
상대가 연결을 끊으면 `BattleResult.DISCONNECT`(부전승)을 내가 제출하지만, 상대는 아무것도 제출 못 한다. 이는 의도된 동작. 상대의 패배는 기록되지 않는다. MVP에서는 허용 범위.

**3. 네트워크 오류 시 UI 처리**
`create()`의 fire-and-forget 제출이 실패해도 UI에 영향 없도록 `.catch()` 처리. `BattleLeaderboardScene`의 로딩 실패는 `LeaderboardScene`과 동일하게 에러 텍스트 표시.

**4. View vs. 직접 JOIN**
`battle_leaderboard_view`를 사용하면 `leaderboard-top`의 `profiles!inner(initials)` JOIN 패턴과 동일하게 유지된다. View에 `initials`가 포함되어 있어 Edge Function에서 추가 JOIN 불필요.

방 생성/참가/카운트다운/게임 시작 흐름\n  - 특수 똥 수집 → 상대 화면에 똥 출현 확인\n  - 상대 점수 실시간 갱신 확인\n  - 한 쪽 사망 → 상대 즉시 승리 확인\n  - 탭 닫기 → 상대 부전승 확인\n  - 캐릭터 능력 정상 작동 확인\n\n## 핵심 파일 참조\n\n| 파일 | 역할 |\n|------|------|\n| `src/scenes/GameScene.ts` | 상속 대상, private→protected 변경 |\n| `src/types/GameMode.ts` | BATTLE 모드 상수 추가 |\n| `src/utils/supabase.ts` | Supabase 클라이언트 (battleChannel에서 import) |\n| `src/abilities/types.ts` | GameSceneAPI — BattleGameScene 호환 확인 |\n| `src/objects/Poop.ts` | 상대 똥 생성 시 reinit() 패턴 참고 |\n| `src/objects/PoolablePoopBase.ts` | recycle() 패턴 참고 |\n| `src/config/poop.ts` | POOP_CONFIG — fallSpeed 계산 참고 |\n| `src/scenes/ModeSelectScene.ts` | 대전 버튼 추가 위치 |\n\n## 주의사항\n\n- TypeScript: `type` import, enum 금지, `as const` 사용\n- Object Pool 패턴 유지: 상대 똥도 `this.poops.get()` + `reinit()` 사용\n- 채널 정리: 씬 전환 시 `battleChannel.destroy()` 호출 필수\n- DB 마이그레이션: MVP에서는 생략 (대전 전적 저장은 추후 확장)",
  "new_string": "## 핵심 설계 결정\n\n- **랭킹 기준**: 총 승리수(wins + disconnects) DESC → 승률 DESC (동점 처리)\n- **전적 단위**: 유저당 1행 (`battle_records`), 승/패/부전승 카운터만 증가\n- **부전승(DISCONNECT)**: `disconnects` 별도 저장, 랭킹에서는 승리로 취급\n- **안티치트 수준**: MVP — 클라이언트 신뢰 방식 (솔로 모드의 세션 타임 검증 불필요, 경제적 이득 없음)\n- **중복 제출 방지**: `BattleResultScene`에 `resultSubmitted` 플래그\n- **기존 패턴 재사용**: Edge Function 구조/배포 플래그는 `leaderboard-submit`과 동일\n\n## 구현 순서\n\n### Step 1: DB Migration — `supabase/migrations/20260321_battle_records.sql`\n\n```sql\n-- 전적 테이블 (유저당 1행)\nCREATE TABLE public.battle_records (\n  user_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  wins        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  losses      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  disconnects INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  updated_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.battle_records ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- 읽기 공개 (랭킹 조회), 쓰기는 service_role만 (정책 없음)\nCREATE POLICY \"battle_records_select_public\"\n  ON public.battle_records FOR SELECT USING (true);\n\n-- 원자적 upsert + rank 반환 함수\n-- (Supabase JS SDK로 wins+disconnects 복합 정렬/집계 불가 → DB 함수로 해결)\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION increment_battle_record(p_user_id UUID, p_column TEXT)\nRETURNS TABLE(wins INTEGER, losses INTEGER, disconnects INTEGER, rank BIGINT)\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER AS $$\nDECLARE v_wins INT; v_losses INT; v_disconnects INT; v_rank BIGINT;\nBEGIN\n  INSERT INTO public.battle_records (user_id) VALUES (p_user_id)\n  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO NOTHING;\n  EXECUTE format('UPDATE public.battle_records SET %I = %I + 1, updated_at = NOW() WHERE user_id = $1', p_column, p_column) USING p_user_id;\n  SELECT br.wins, br.losses, br.disconnects INTO v_wins, v_losses, v_disconnects\n  FROM public.battle_records br WHERE br.user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 INTO v_rank\n  FROM public.battle_records br WHERE (br.wins + br.disconnects) > (v_wins + v_disconnects);\n  RETURN QUERY SELECT v_wins, v_losses, v_disconnects, v_rank;\nEND; $$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION increment_battle_record FROM PUBLIC;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION increment_battle_record TO service_role;\n\n-- 랭킹 뷰 (복합 정렬 + win_rate 계산 포함)\nCREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.battle_leaderboard_view AS\nSELECT\n  br.user_id,\n  br.wins, br.losses, br.disconnects,\n  (br.wins + br.disconnects) AS total_wins,\n  CASE WHEN (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects) = 0 THEN 0.0\n    ELSE ROUND((br.wins + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC / (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC * 100, 1)\n  END AS win_rate,\n  p.initials\nFROM public.battle_records br\nJOIN public.profiles p ON p.id = br.user_id\nORDER BY (br.wins + br.disconnects) DESC,\n  CASE WHEN (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects) = 0 THEN 0.0\n    ELSE (br.wins + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC / (br.wins + br.losses + br.disconnects)::NUMERIC\n  END DESC;\n\nGRANT SELECT ON public.battle_leaderboard_view TO anon, authenticated;\n\n-- 현재 유저 rank 계산용 함수\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_battle_rank(p_total_wins INTEGER)\nRETURNS BIGINT LANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $$\n  SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.battle_records WHERE (wins + disconnects) > p_total_wins;\n$$;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION get_battle_rank TO anon, authenticated, service_role;\n```\n\n### Step 2: Edge Function — `supabase/functions/battle-result-submit/index.ts`\n\n패턴: `leaderboard-submit`과 동일 (JWT 수동 검증 → service_role로 DB 작업)\n\n- Input: `{ result: 'win' | 'lose' | 'disconnect' }`\n- 검증: JWT 필수, result 값 검증\n- DB: `supabaseAdmin.rpc('increment_battle_record', { p_user_id, p_column })`\n- 반환: `{ success, wins, losses, disconnects, winRate, rank }`\n\n배포: `supabase functions deploy battle-result-submit --no-verify-jwt`\n\n### Step 3: Edge Function — `supabase/functions/battle-leaderboard-top/index.ts`\n\n패턴: `leaderboard-top`과 동일 (JWT 선택적)\n\n- Input: `{ limit?: number }` (기본 20, 최대 100)\n- DB: `from('battle_leaderboard_view').select('*').limit(limit)`\n- 현재 유저 순위: `rpc('get_battle_rank', { p_total_wins })` 로 계산\n- 반환: `{ success, leaderboard: BattleRecordEntry[], currentUserRank }`\n\n배포: `supabase functions deploy battle-leaderboard-top --no-verify-jwt`\n\n### Step 4: `src/utils/battleLeaderboard.ts` (신규)\n\n`skor.ts`의 `getSession()` → `access_token` 주입 패턴 재사용.\n\n```typescript\nexport async function submitBattleResult(result: BattleResult): Promise<SubmitResponse>\nexport async function getBattleLeaderboard(limit?: number): Promise<LeaderboardResponse>\n```\n\n인터페이스:\n```typescript\nexport interface BattleRecordEntry {\n  rank: number; userId: string; userName: string;\n  wins: number; losses: number; disconnects: number;\n  totalWins: number; winRate: number;\n}\n```\n\n### Step 5: `src/scenes/BattleLeaderboardScene.ts` (신규)\n\n`LeaderboardScene.ts`를 템플릿으로 사용 (requestId 중복 요청 방지 패턴 불필요 — 탭 없는 단일 뷰).\n\n**UI 레이아웃:**\n```\ny=40   🏆 배틀 전적\ny=90   내 전적: W승 L패 D부전승 | 승률 XX% (currentUserRank 있을 때)\ny=115  ─── 구분선 ───\ny=140  헤더: 순위 | 이름 | 승 | 패 | 승률\ny=175~ 항목 (35px 간격, 최대 20행) — 현재 유저 행은 노란 배경 하이라이트\ny=555  ← 뒤로가기 → BattleMatchScene\n```\n\n`supabase.auth.getUser().then(...)` 로 userId 백그라운드 조회 (create()는 동기).\n로딩/오류 상태 텍스트 표시 (`LeaderboardScene` 동일 패턴).\n\n### Step 6: `src/scenes/BattleResultScene.ts` 수정\n\n1. 클래스에 `private resultSubmitted = false` 추가\n2. `init()`에서 `this.resultSubmitted = false` 초기화\n3. `create()` 끝에 fire-and-forget 제출:\n```typescript\nif (!this.resultSubmitted) {\n  this.resultSubmitted = true;\n  submitBattleResult(this.result).catch((err) => console.warn('Battle result submit failed:', err));\n}\n```\n4. 버튼 레이아웃 조정 (3버튼):\n   - y=450: ⚔️ 재대전 (height 50)\n   - y=510: 🏆 전적 보기 → BattleLeaderboardScene (height 50, 초록 0x27ae60)\n   - y=565: 메인 메뉴 (텍스트 링크 스타일)\n\n### Step 7: `src/scenes/BattleMatchScene.ts` 수정\n\n`showMenu()` 내 y=450에 텍스트 링크 추가 (`this.ui(...)` 래퍼로 uiGroup 등록):\n```typescript\nconst rankLink = this.ui(this.add.text(200, 450, '🏆 전적 보기', { ... }));\nrankLink.on('pointerdown', () => this.scene.start('BattleLeaderboardScene'));\n```\n\n### Step 8: `src/main.ts` 수정\n\n`BattleLeaderboardScene` import 및 scene 배열 끝에 추가.\n\n## 핵심 파일 참조\n\n| 파일 | 역할 |\n|------|------|\n| `supabase/functions/leaderboard-submit/index.ts` | JWT 수동 검증 + service_role
